# Heron evaluation certification policy, Q3 2025 close

This policy governs the certified scorecard for the Q3 2025 candidate checkpoints. Where sources overlap or disagree, the rules below and the per-source notes decide; a value is never taken from a file just because it is newer, more complete or labelled final.
## Source authority

The baseline model is osprey-13b-prod. Candidate models are the model aliases of the run registry.

Production result sources are results_fleet_a.log, results_fleet_b.log, results_fleet_c.log, code_samples.jsonl, judge_verdicts.jsonl, scores_shard1.parquet, scores_shard2.parquet, eval_store.db, legacy_results.txt, intent_predictions.csv and rerun_results.jsonl. The baseline reference is baseline_reference.csv.

The following files are not authoritative for results or registry values, with the single exceptions stated: scores_staging.parquet (only the rows of jobs the errata register marks RESTORE), eval_store_mirror.db (only its void_requests table), judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl (only the presentation field), checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json (only entries with ema true), run_registry_export.xml (only the legacy_no map), locale_codes_legacy.tsv (only rows of kind map), gold_labels_corrected.csv (only the dispute_ticket to item map) and scorecard_prelim.xlsx (only the suite_weight column, which is the board-adopted leaderboard weight of the item's suite).
## Identifier grammar

Job ids are J followed by 7 digits. Item ids are a suite prefix (ARI, COD, INT, SAF, MCQ, PRF, LCX, MLG), a hyphen and 5 digits. Host names are node- and 4 digits. Checkpoint ids are ck- and 10 lower-case hex digits. Change tickets are CHG- and 5 digits; dispute tickets are DSP- and 5 digits; legacy item codes are LC and 6 digits; row ids are r and 7 digits. Canonical case is as written here. Values can arrive in the wrong case, without zero padding, with surrounding spaces or no-break spaces, or with a Cyrillic look-alike letter; the confusables are А for A, В for B, С for C, Е for E, Н for H, Ј for J, К for K, М for M, О for O, Р for P, Т for T, Х for X, а for a, с for c, е for e, о for o, р for p, х for x.
## Model aliases and checkpoints

Each model alias is certified on exactly one checkpoint: the checkpoint of the latest tag event (promotion or rollback) for that alias that took effect. A tag event takes effect at the Date of its APPROVED message in evalops_notifications.mbox when that Date is at or before the cutoff 2025-10-03T18:00:00Z and no REJECTED message exists for the ticket. Events without an approval, with a rejection, or approved after the cutoff have no effect; they are counted as voided promotions of the alias.
## Job dispositions

Every registered job gets exactly one disposition, decided in this order (the first that applies): voided (errata job void list), staging_host (the job's host is in fleet s in the inventory entry for the ISO week of the submission instant), stale_checkpoint (the job ran a checkpoint other than its alias's certified checkpoint), outside_window (submission instant outside 2025-07-01T00:00:00Z inclusive to 2025-10-01T00:00:00Z exclusive), maintenance (submitted inside a maintenance window of its host, DTSTART inclusive, DTEND exclusive, without an exemption in the review minutes), superseded (an approved rerun replaces it), rerun_denied (it is a rerun without an approval) and otherwise authoritative. The submission instant is the earliest SUBMIT line of the job in harness_events.log after zone and clock correction. For each model, suite and shard exactly one job is authoritative.
## Item eligibility

An item is ineligible for the first of these reasons that applies: retired (suite manifest), duplicate (a non-canonical member of a dedup cluster), contaminated (a live contamination scan at or above the suite threshold), out_of_scope (held out in Appendix A). Otherwise it is eligible. Only eligible items are scored.
## Release gates

A candidate is released when all four gates hold: its leaderboard aggregate is at least the baseline aggregate (compared exactly before rounding); none of its gated suites (arith, mcq, longctx, multiling) is regressed; its multiple-choice expected calibration error is at most 0.120; its safety AUROC is at least 0.700.
## Per-source notes

baseline_reference.csv:
- A baseline row repeated verbatim is one row.
- Item id values in baseline_reference.csv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in baseline_reference.csv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in baseline_reference.csv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in baseline_reference.csv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in baseline_reference.csv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Legacy item code values in baseline_reference.csv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Legacy item code values in baseline_reference.csv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Legacy item code values in baseline_reference.csv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Legacy item code values in baseline_reference.csv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Legacy item code values in baseline_reference.csv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Baseline rows with status withdrawn are out of scope.

certification_policy.md:
- Item id values in certification_policy.md can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in certification_policy.md can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in certification_policy.md can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in certification_policy.md can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in certification_policy.md can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.

checkpoint_lineage.json:
- Lower-case hex checkpoint ids are already canonical.
- A checkpoint is the certified checkpoint of an alias only through an approved tag event; lineage alone certifies nothing.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage.json can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage.json can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage.json can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage.json can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage.json can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- An entry repeated verbatim is one entry.
- family can be written 7B, 13B, 34B, OSP-13 or heron-7b style; it maps to the model families of the policy.
- EMA checkpoints are not registered in checkpoint_lineage.json; they appear only in the lineage snapshot, whose EMA entries are authoritative.
- Parent ids follow the same checkpoint id grammar and variants as checkpoint ids.
- Where checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json carries a conflicting copy of a checkpoint_lineage.json fact, the checkpoint_lineage.json value stands; checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json:
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Checkpoint id values in checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Entries with ema true and an ema_of parent are the only registration of EMA checkpoints and are authoritative for them.
- An EMA checkpoint takes family, tokenizer and context window from its ema_of parent in checkpoint_lineage.json.
- Snapshot fields agree with the registry for most checkpoints and differ for some; the registry wins for every non-EMA field.
- The snapshot marks some checkpoints best; best is a dashboard label with no certification meaning.
- Snapshot context windows reflect a planned extension that was never deployed; they are not used.
- snapshot_at values are epoch milliseconds.
- An entry repeated verbatim is one entry.
- EMA entries look like duplicates of their parents (same step); they are distinct checkpoints.
- Snapshot family names use marketing names (Heron Small, Heron Medium, Heron Large, Osprey).
- Snapshot entries whose id is not in the registry and that are not EMA entries are scratch checkpoints and are ignored.
- The snapshot carries more columns (eval_ready, owner); extra columns do not make it authoritative.
- Some EMA ids are written with an ema/ prefix that is not part of the id.
- Key order differs between entries.
- A footnote in the snapshot header limits its validity to EMA registrations.
- The snapshot is dated after checkpoint_lineage.json; for non-EMA checkpoints the registry entry still wins.
- ema_of null on a non-EMA entry is expected.
- Older snapshot entries use the key ckpt instead of id.
- The owner field says training-infra for every entry; ownership is not authority.
- Snapshot entries carry no parent field; lineage parents come from checkpoint_lineage.json.
- eval_ready is true for every entry; it is a dashboard flag with no certification meaning.
- Snapshot run names can be upper-cased.
- Snapshot steps are in thousands.
- The snapshot lists alias pointers as of its own date; aliases are decided only by approved tag events.
- Snapshot entries flagged withdrawn are void.
- Run names can contain spaces.
- A checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json row that restates a checkpoint_lineage.json fact is superseded by checkpoint_lineage.json for that fact.
- A checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json row that restates a job_configs.toml fact is superseded by job_configs.toml for that fact.
- A checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json row that restates a model_tags.yaml fact is superseded by model_tags.yaml for that fact.
- A checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json row that restates a run_registry.xml fact is superseded by run_registry.xml for that fact.

dedup_clusters.tsv:
- Within a cluster the canonical member is the lowest item id after canonicalisation; every other member is a duplicate.
- Cluster ids can be zero-padded to four digits.
- An entry of dedup_clusters.tsv repeated verbatim is one entry, not a conflict.
- Entries of dedup_clusters.tsv whose item id is not registered in item_registry.csv are ignored.
- Item id values in dedup_clusters.tsv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in dedup_clusters.tsv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in dedup_clusters.tsv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in dedup_clusters.tsv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in dedup_clusters.tsv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Members with low similarity in a multi-member cluster are still duplicates; cluster membership, not similarity, decides.
- A cluster id of 0 means singleton; singletons that share cluster 0 are not duplicates of one another.
- A later revision can move an item out of its cluster (cluster 0); the highest revision decides membership.
- Cluster ids repeat across suites; a cluster is the pair (suite prefix, cluster id).

errata_register.docx:
- VOID-BASELINE entries name a baseline item (item id) and revision date.
- An entry repeated verbatim is one entry.
- VOID-JOB entries void every row of the job.
- Job ids in the job void list can be in the wrong case.
- RESTORE entries name jobs whose primary rows were lost; the job's rows come from the staging snapshot.
- Row id values in errata_register.docx can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Row id values in errata_register.docx can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Row id values in errata_register.docx can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Row id values in errata_register.docx can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- A row void entry names its source file; it voids that row in that file only.
- Row id values in errata_register.docx can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Source files can be named without extension.
- An UNDO entry cancels an earlier entry with the same target.
- VOID-ENTRY rows for document tables name the file, the key and the revision (or snapshot or seq) of the voided entry.
- VOID-ENTRY rows for host inventory and clock files name the file, the key and the revision (or snapshot or seq) of the voided entry.
- VOID-ENTRY rows for item registry, gold, dedup, context and slice files name the file, the key and the revision (or snapshot or seq) of the voided entry.
- VOID-ENTRY rows for locale, manifest, scoring parameter and alias files name the file, the key and the revision (or snapshot or seq) of the voided entry.
- VOID-ENTRY rows for job, checkpoint and tag registries name the file, the key and the revision (or snapshot or seq) of the voided entry.

- Job id values in eval_store.db can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in eval_store.db can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Sandbox failures that exited normally are listed in the void_requests table of the replica database; those eval_store.db rows are void even though the replica is otherwise stale.

- Replica values agree with the primary store on most rows and differ on the rest; the primary store wins.
- eval_store_mirror.db is a replica snapshot taken on 2025-09-12; rows after the snapshot are missing and its values are not authoritative.
- The replica's created timestamp is later than the primary's; that does not make it newer data.
- The replica's meta table says role=replica; replica rows never replace primary rows.
- Replica rows stop at the 2025-09-12 snapshot instant; absent later rows are not deletions.
- The replica's void_requests table is the only record of sandbox failures that exited normally; those voids apply to code_samples.jsonl and eval_store.db.
- Row id values in eval_store_mirror.db can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.

- A eval_store_mirror.db row that restates a job_configs.toml fact is superseded by job_configs.toml for that fact.
- A eval_store_mirror.db row that restates a rerun_results.jsonl fact is superseded by rerun_results.jsonl for that fact.
- A eval_store_mirror.db row that restates a results_fleet_c.log fact is superseded by results_fleet_c.log for that fact.

evalops_notifications.mbox:
- APPROVED and REJECTED messages decide tag tickets; an approval takes effect at its Date if that is at or before the cutoff 2025-10-03T18:00:00Z.
- Forwarded copies (Subject starting Fwd:) repeat an earlier message; the original Date counts.
- Job id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Ticket id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Ticket id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Ticket id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Ticket id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Ticket id values in evalops_notifications.mbox can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Approvals dated after the cutoff have no effect; an approval exactly at the cutoff counts.
- A REJECTED message cancels its ticket even if an approval for the same ticket came earlier.
- Where judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl carries a conflicting copy of a evalops_notifications.mbox fact, the evalops_notifications.mbox value stands; judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

field_precedence.html:
- Only jobs that carry a second copy can be of the bridge class; a bridge entry for a single-copy job is a data-entry error and is treated as standard.
- The bridge class reverses the default winner for every field of the job.
- Class can be written std, standard, br or bridge.
- Class values can be upper-cased.
- A table row repeated verbatim in field_precedence.html is one row.
- Class rows for unregistered jobs are ignored.
- Job id values in field_precedence.html can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in field_precedence.html can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in field_precedence.html can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in field_precedence.html can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in field_precedence.html can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Where a table in field_precedence.html lists a key twice, the row with the higher revision wins.

gold_labels.tsv:
- A gold row repeated verbatim is one row.
- Entries of gold_labels.tsv whose item id is not registered in item_registry.csv are ignored.
- Item id values in gold_labels.tsv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in gold_labels.tsv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in gold_labels.tsv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in gold_labels.tsv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in gold_labels.tsv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Where gold_labels_corrected.csv carries a conflicting copy of a gold_labels.tsv fact, the gold_labels.tsv value stands; gold_labels_corrected.csv is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where locale_codes_legacy.tsv carries a conflicting copy of a gold_labels.tsv fact, the gold_labels.tsv value stands; locale_codes_legacy.tsv is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

gold_labels_corrected.csv:
- Corrected gold agrees with gold_labels.tsv on most items and differs on the rest; only upheld grader errata change a gold value.
- A correction equal to the current gold is a confirmation and changes nothing.
- The file starts with a byte order mark.
- Corrections dated after the cutoff are out of the close.
- A ticket maps to an item only for errata entries upheld after the ticket's corrected_at.
- A row repeated verbatim is one row.
- Text corrections are CSV-quoted.
- Corrected values are internally consistent with the preliminary scorecard; that agreement is not evidence.
- Every row says status FINAL; the corrections were never upheld by the grader errata process.
- Corrections fix some values that look wrong but are valid per the scoring parameters (tolerance-equivalent answers).
- Some rows name item ids that are not registered.
- The file covers every item including preference items; coverage does not make it authoritative.
- A second header renames gold_corrected to answer_fixed.
- Numeric corrections from the Munich desk use a comma decimal mark.
- corrected_at dates are later than the gold file revisions; later does not make a correction authoritative.
- An empty correction means no change was proposed.
- Trusting this file for values breaks the errata reconciliation; ignoring it entirely loses the ticket map; the errata rule takes values from the errata and items from this file.
- Where a ticket appears in both this file and the errata, the errata decide the value and this file decides only the item.
- Corrections round numeric gold to whole numbers.
- Safety corrections are written as percentages.
- The header note limits the file to proposals under discussion.
- corrected_at can be a spreadsheet serial date.
- Intent corrections use taxonomy v2 names whatever the item's taxonomy.
- Item id values in gold_labels_corrected.csv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in gold_labels_corrected.csv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in gold_labels_corrected.csv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in gold_labels_corrected.csv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in gold_labels_corrected.csv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- A gold_labels_corrected.csv row that restates a gold_labels.tsv fact is superseded by gold_labels.tsv for that fact.
- A gold_labels_corrected.csv row that restates a grader_errata.xml fact is superseded by grader_errata.xml for that fact.
- A gold_labels_corrected.csv row that restates a item_registry.csv fact is superseded by item_registry.csv for that fact.
- A gold_labels_corrected.csv row that restates a scoring_parameters.xlsx fact is superseded by scoring_parameters.xlsx for that fact.
- Dispute tickets can be written in lower case or without zero padding.
- dispute_ticket is the only crosswalk from dispute tickets (DSP-nnnnn) to item ids; grader errata name tickets, not items.

grader_errata.xml:
- Errata decided after the cutoff are not applied to this close.
- A confirmed erratum re-states the existing gold and changes nothing.
- Dispute ticket values in grader_errata.xml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Dispute ticket values in grader_errata.xml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Dispute ticket values in grader_errata.xml can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Dispute ticket values in grader_errata.xml can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Dispute ticket values in grader_errata.xml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Among upheld and withdrawn errata for one ticket, the latest decided instant wins.
- A note attribute mentioning another ticket does not apply to it.
- status values can be upper-cased.
- Errata name dispute tickets; the ticket-to-item map is the dispute_ticket column of the corrected-gold export.
- An upheld erratum replaces the gold value with its value attribute.
- Intent erratum values are written in taxonomy v2 names.
- Where gold_labels_corrected.csv carries a conflicting copy of a grader_errata.xml fact, the grader_errata.xml value stands; gold_labels_corrected.csv is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- A withdrawn erratum changes nothing, even when it is the latest entry for its ticket.

harness_events.log:
- SUBMIT lines for job ids absent from the run registry are ignored.
- Host name values in harness_events.log can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in harness_events.log can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Host name values in harness_events.log can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Host name values in harness_events.log can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in harness_events.log can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Job id values in harness_events.log can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in harness_events.log can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in harness_events.log can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in harness_events.log can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in harness_events.log can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- SUBMIT lines for jobs submitted before the scheduler migration can name the job by its retired number; the export maps it.
- A submission inside a maintenance window of its host does not count unless the review minutes exempt the job.
- Where run_registry_export.xml carries a conflicting copy of a harness_events.log fact, the harness_events.log value stands; run_registry_export.xml is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl carries a conflicting copy of a harness_events.log fact, the harness_events.log value stands; judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- The certification window is half-open: 2025-07-01T00:00:00Z inclusive to 2025-10-01T00:00:00Z exclusive.

host_inventory.ini:
- An entry repeated verbatim inside one snapshot section is one entry.
- Fleet codes can be long names (fleet-a, prod-b, stage); stage and staging both mean the staging fleet s.
- Host name values in host_inventory.ini can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in host_inventory.ini can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.

- Host name values in host_inventory.ini can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- A host whose week entry says fleet s is a staging host for every job submitted that week.

intent_predictions.csv:
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv whose job id is not in the run registry (only the retired scheduler export lists it) do not count.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv for items with a counted contamination hit at or above the suite threshold carry no score.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv for items that are non-canonical members of a dedup cluster carry no score.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv for held-out items carry no score; held-out is the last exclusion reason.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv for items retired in the suite manifest carry no score.

- Item id values in intent_predictions.csv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in intent_predictions.csv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in intent_predictions.csv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in intent_predictions.csv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv whose job was submitted inside a maintenance window of its host without an exemption do not count.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv whose job was submitted outside the half-open certification window do not count.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv whose job ran a checkpoint other than the one its model alias certifies do not count.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv whose job was replaced by an approved rerun do not count.
- Rows of intent_predictions.csv whose job is listed in the errata job void list do not count.
- Job id values in intent_predictions.csv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in intent_predictions.csv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in intent_predictions.csv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in intent_predictions.csv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in intent_predictions.csv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Where scorecard_prelim.xlsx carries a conflicting copy of a intent_predictions.csv fact, the intent_predictions.csv value stands; scorecard_prelim.xlsx is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where scores_staging.parquet carries a conflicting copy of a intent_predictions.csv fact, the intent_predictions.csv value stands; scores_staging.parquet is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

item_registry.csv:
- An entry repeated verbatim (same item, revision and values) is one entry, not a conflict.
- An item id is registered only when it is listed both in item_registry.csv and in the shard map of the metric definitions; other item_registry.csv rows are not items.
- Item id values in item_registry.csv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in item_registry.csv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in item_registry.csv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in item_registry.csv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in item_registry.csv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Revision 0 entries are unpublished drafts and are ignored.
- Entries whose suite is sandbox are harness self-tests and are not items.
- Entries with revision below 3 name the suite by its legacy name (arithmetic, codegen, intents, safety_flags, multiple_choice, pairwise, long_context, multilingual).
- Where gold_labels_corrected.csv carries a conflicting copy of a item_registry.csv fact, the item_registry.csv value stands; gold_labels_corrected.csv is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Suite names can carry trailing spaces.

job_configs.toml:
- Configs for jobs absent from the run registry are ignored.
- harness can be written 3.2, h3.2 or harness-3.2.
- harness can be upper-cased (H3.2).
- Job id values in job_configs.toml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in job_configs.toml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.

- Job id values in job_configs.toml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Harness h4.0 uses taxonomy v2 and grader g3; h3.1 and h3.2 use taxonomy v1 and grader g2.
- Where run_registry_export.xml carries a conflicting copy of a job_configs.toml fact, the job_configs.toml value stands; run_registry_export.xml is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where eval_store_mirror.db carries a conflicting copy of a job_configs.toml fact, the job_configs.toml value stands; eval_store_mirror.db is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

- Where checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json carries a conflicting copy of a job_configs.toml fact, the job_configs.toml value stands; checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

- Job id values in judge_verdicts.jsonl can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.

- Job id values in judge_verdicts.jsonl can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in judge_verdicts.jsonl can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- A final reviewer verdict in the manual overrides workbook replaces the judge verdict for that job, item and slot; draft reviews do not.
- Where judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl carries a conflicting copy of a judge_verdicts.jsonl fact, the judge_verdicts.jsonl value stands; judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl:

- Item id values in judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl whose job is a rerun without an approval do not count.
- Rows of judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl whose job was submitted inside a maintenance window of its host without an exemption do not count.
- Rows of judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl whose job was submitted outside the half-open certification window do not count.
- Rows of judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl whose job ran a checkpoint other than the one its model alias certifies do not count.

- Job id values in judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rescored verdicts agree with the approved judge on most items and differ on the rest; the approved judge or a final review wins.
- The rescored export covers every preference item including reviewed ones; completeness does not make it authoritative.
- A rescored row repeated verbatim carries no second result; the repeat still counts as a record line in the source audit.
- Rescored rows exist for held-out items too; scope is decided by the policy appendix.
- judge jm-3 is a newer judge model than jm-1 and jm-2; it was not approved for certification.
- Each rescored row carries status final; that label is the rescoring pipeline's own and not a review status.
- The rescored export is newer than judge_verdicts.jsonl; its verdicts come from judge jm-3, which the policy never approved, so they are not used.
- Some rescored rows name jobs that are not preference jobs of the run registry; those rows are ignored.
- Every rescored row says status final; that is the rescoring pipeline's own status, not a review.
- A judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl row that restates a evalops_notifications.mbox fact is superseded by evalops_notifications.mbox for that fact.
- A judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl row that restates a harness_events.log fact is superseded by harness_events.log for that fact.
- A judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl row that restates a job_configs.toml fact is superseded by job_configs.toml for that fact.
- A judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl row that restates a judge_verdicts.jsonl fact is superseded by judge_verdicts.jsonl for that fact.
- A judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl row that restates a manual_overrides.xlsx fact is superseded by manual_overrides.xlsx for that fact.
- A judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl row that restates a rerun_results.jsonl fact is superseded by rerun_results.jsonl for that fact.
- A judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl row that restates a run_registry.xml fact is superseded by run_registry.xml for that fact.

label_aliases.json:
- Some aliases resolve to different classes under v1 and v2; the version is the job's harness taxonomy.
- Aliases ending in ! or containing parentheses are real aliases, not markup.
- Class names in entries can be upper-cased; class names are lower case.
- Class names can carry a trailing space.
- Entries marked deprecated_in v2 are still valid for v1 jobs.
- An alias entry repeated verbatim is one entry.
- Entries whose class is not in the taxonomy are ignored.
- Raw aliases can be stored in decomposed Unicode form; match on NFC.
- Alias matching is exact on the NFC form of the raw label, including case and punctuation; there is no case folding.
- A v2 of null means the alias is unchanged in v2 (same class as v1, renamed if the class was renamed).
- Taxonomy v2 renames hours to opening_hours; certified intent values use v2 names.
- Taxonomy v2 renames reset_password to account_recovery; certified intent values use v2 names.
- Taxonomy v2 renames shipping_cost to delivery_fee; certified intent values use v2 names.

- Rows of legacy_results.txt whose job id is not in the run registry (only the retired scheduler export lists it) do not count.

- Rows of legacy_results.txt for items that are non-canonical members of a dedup cluster carry no score.
- Rows of legacy_results.txt for held-out items carry no score; held-out is the last exclusion reason.
- Rows of legacy_results.txt for items retired in the suite manifest carry no score.
- Item id values in legacy_results.txt can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in legacy_results.txt can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in legacy_results.txt can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in legacy_results.txt can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in legacy_results.txt can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.

- Rows of legacy_results.txt whose job was submitted outside the half-open certification window do not count.
- Rows of legacy_results.txt whose job ran a checkpoint other than the one its model alias certifies do not count.
- Rows of legacy_results.txt whose job was replaced by an approved rerun do not count.
- Rows of legacy_results.txt whose job is listed in the errata job void list do not count.

- Job id values in legacy_results.txt can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.

- Where scorecard_prelim.xlsx carries a conflicting copy of a legacy_results.txt fact, the legacy_results.txt value stands; scorecard_prelim.xlsx is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

locale_codes_legacy.tsv:
- Item id values in locale_codes_legacy.tsv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in locale_codes_legacy.tsv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in locale_codes_legacy.tsv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in locale_codes_legacy.tsv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in locale_codes_legacy.tsv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Legacy item locales agree with prompt_locales.tsv for most items and differ for some.
- A mapping row whose source and target look alike (in to id) is correct.
- Legacy minimum support is a strict greater-than; the slice definitions use at least.
- Support counts in the legacy table are in hundreds.
- The migration date is written as a spreadsheet serial.
- Mappings take effect from the locale migration date, which is before the window.
- Repeated rows are one row.
- Notes after a tab are free text.
- The file is Latin-1 in places.
- Legacy item locales agree with the preliminary scorecard's language split; that agreement is not evidence.
- Rows for unregistered items are ignored.
- The legacy table lists a locale for every item; prompt_locales.tsv is the authority for item locales.
- A second header swaps the columns.
- Item ids in the legacy table are lower case.
- Legacy support ratios use a comma decimal mark.
- The legacy table's updated column is later than prompt_locales.tsv revisions; it is an archive refresh date.
- A locale of ?? means unknown.
- Taking item locales from the legacy table breaks the per-language support counts; ignoring the table leaves deprecated codes unresolved; only the map rows are used.
- Rare languages in the legacy table are flagged; the flag has no effect.
- For deprecated codes the legacy map decides the language; for item locales prompt_locales.tsv decides.
- Mapped codes keep the region subtag of the original tag; only the language changes.
- Legacy locales use country codes as languages for some items (BR for pt).
- Legacy support ratios are rounded.
- The header comment marks the item rows as historical.
- Legacy locales for some languages carry a script subtag.
- A mapping target of - means the code is retired with no successor.
- updated is epoch seconds.
- Rows marked retired in the legacy table are void.
- Rows of kind map give the deprecated-code mapping (iw to he, in to id, jw to jv, ji to yi, mo to ro); this mapping is authoritative.
- Mapping codes can be upper case.
- A locale_codes_legacy.tsv row that restates a gold_labels.tsv fact is superseded by gold_labels.tsv for that fact.
- A locale_codes_legacy.tsv row that restates a prompt_locales.tsv fact is superseded by prompt_locales.tsv for that fact.
- A locale_codes_legacy.tsv row that restates a rerun_results.jsonl fact is superseded by rerun_results.jsonl for that fact.
- A locale_codes_legacy.tsv row that restates a slice_definitions.yaml fact is superseded by slice_definitions.yaml for that fact.

maintenance_windows.ics:
- VALUE=DATE events (all-day) are planning placeholders with no effect.
- STATUS:CANCELLED events are not maintenance windows.
- A maintenance window covers DTSTART inclusive to DTEND exclusive.
- Host name values in maintenance_windows.ics can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in maintenance_windows.ics can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Host name values in maintenance_windows.ics can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Host name values in maintenance_windows.ics can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in maintenance_windows.ics can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.

manual_overrides.xlsx:
- A final review equal to the judge verdict confirms it.
- Only reviews with status final replace a judge verdict; draft reviews are ignored.
- A review row repeated verbatim is one review.
- Item id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Job id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in manual_overrides.xlsx can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- When a slot has several final reviews the latest reviewed_at wins.
- Reviewers record verdicts as candidate, baseline or tie for the slot pair; they are already in the candidate's perspective.
- status can be written FINAL or Final.
- A verdict of tie or = is a tie.
- Verdict words can be capitalised.
- Where run_registry_export.xml carries a conflicting copy of a manual_overrides.xlsx fact, the manual_overrides.xlsx value stands; run_registry_export.xml is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl carries a conflicting copy of a manual_overrides.xlsx fact, the manual_overrides.xlsx value stands; judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

methodology_appendix.pdf:
- Legacy codes can be written with a space after LC (LC 457842).
- A table row repeated verbatim in methodology_appendix.pdf is one row.
- Crosswalk rows for unregistered items are ignored.
- An asterisk after an item id marks an item added this quarter; it is a footnote marker, not part of the id.
- Item id values in methodology_appendix.pdf can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in methodology_appendix.pdf can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in methodology_appendix.pdf can carry leading or trailing spaces; strip them before joining.
- A legacy code maps to exactly one item; a code listed with two items keeps the higher revision.
- Legacy item code values in methodology_appendix.pdf can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Legacy item code values in methodology_appendix.pdf can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Legacy item code values in methodology_appendix.pdf can carry leading or trailing spaces; strip them before joining.
- Where a table in methodology_appendix.pdf lists a key twice, the row with the higher revision wins.

metric_definitions.pdf:
- A table row repeated verbatim in metric_definitions.pdf is one row.
- Shard map rows for unregistered items are ignored.
- Item id values in metric_definitions.pdf can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in metric_definitions.pdf can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in metric_definitions.pdf can carry leading or trailing spaces; strip them before joining.
- Where a table in metric_definitions.pdf lists a key twice, the row with the higher revision wins.
- Suite names in the shard map can be legacy names.
- Suite names in the shard map can be upper-cased.

model_tags.yaml:
- The baseline alias osprey-13b-prod follows the same tag rules as candidate aliases.
- An approval dated exactly at the cutoff counts.
- Checkpoint id values in model_tags.yaml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Checkpoint id values in model_tags.yaml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Checkpoint id values in model_tags.yaml can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Checkpoint id values in model_tags.yaml can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Checkpoint id values in model_tags.yaml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- A certification tag event takes effect at the Date of the approval message for its ticket in the operations mailbox; rejected tickets and approvals after the cutoff have no effect.
- The -ema alias points to EMA checkpoints registered only in the lineage snapshot.
- Ticket id values in model_tags.yaml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Ticket id values in model_tags.yaml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Ticket id values in model_tags.yaml can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Ticket id values in model_tags.yaml can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Ticket id values in model_tags.yaml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Among effective events of a tag the one with the latest effective time wins.
- nightly-* tags are not certification aliases and are out of scope, even when the tag name contains a model alias.
- A promotion with no approval message at all has no effect.
- A retire action removes a nightly tag; it never applies to certification aliases.
- A rollback event points the tag back to an earlier checkpoint; it is a tag event like a promotion and needs its own approval.
- Tag names can be written in any case.
- Where checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json carries a conflicting copy of a model_tags.yaml fact, the model_tags.yaml value stands; checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

prompt_locales.tsv:
- A bare en tag is English exactly like en-US; it is not an unknown locale.
- Deprecated ISO 639 codes (iw, in, ...) are mapped through the legacy locale code table.
- An entry of prompt_locales.tsv repeated verbatim is one entry, not a conflict.
- Entries of prompt_locales.tsv whose item id is not registered in item_registry.csv are ignored.
- Item id values in prompt_locales.tsv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in prompt_locales.tsv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in prompt_locales.tsv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in prompt_locales.tsv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in prompt_locales.tsv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Numeric region subtags (es-419) do not change the language.
- Private-use subtags (x-...) carry no language information.
- Some tags carry a script subtag (sr-Latn); the language is still the first subtag.
- Locale tags use - or _ and any case; the language is the part before the separator, lower-cased.
- Where locale_codes_legacy.tsv carries a conflicting copy of a prompt_locales.tsv fact, the prompt_locales.tsv value stands; locale_codes_legacy.tsv is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

rerun_results.jsonl:
- Rows of rerun_results.jsonl for items with a counted contamination hit at or above the suite threshold carry no score.
- Rows of rerun_results.jsonl for items that are non-canonical members of a dedup cluster carry no score.
- Rows of rerun_results.jsonl for held-out items carry no score; held-out is the last exclusion reason.
- Rows of rerun_results.jsonl for items retired in the suite manifest carry no score.
- Item id values in rerun_results.jsonl can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in rerun_results.jsonl can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in rerun_results.jsonl can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in rerun_results.jsonl can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in rerun_results.jsonl can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of rerun_results.jsonl whose job was replaced by an approved rerun do not count.
- Job id values in rerun_results.jsonl can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in rerun_results.jsonl can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in rerun_results.jsonl can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in rerun_results.jsonl can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in rerun_results.jsonl can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Reviewer verdicts in manual_overrides.xlsx apply only to judge_verdicts.jsonl rows; rerun verdicts are never overridden.
- Where locale_codes_legacy.tsv carries a conflicting copy of a rerun_results.jsonl fact, the rerun_results.jsonl value stands; locale_codes_legacy.tsv is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where eval_store_mirror.db carries a conflicting copy of a rerun_results.jsonl fact, the rerun_results.jsonl value stands; eval_store_mirror.db is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl carries a conflicting copy of a rerun_results.jsonl fact, the rerun_results.jsonl value stands; judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

results_fleet_a.log:
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log whose job id is not in the run registry (only the retired scheduler export lists it) do not count.
- Host name values in results_fleet_a.log can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in results_fleet_a.log can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Host name values in results_fleet_a.log can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Host name values in results_fleet_a.log can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in results_fleet_a.log can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log for items with a counted contamination hit at or above the suite threshold carry no score.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log for items that are non-canonical members of a dedup cluster carry no score.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log for held-out items carry no score; held-out is the last exclusion reason.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log for items retired in the suite manifest carry no score.
- Item id values in results_fleet_a.log can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in results_fleet_a.log can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in results_fleet_a.log can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in results_fleet_a.log can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in results_fleet_a.log can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log whose job was submitted inside a maintenance window of its host without an exemption do not count.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log whose job was submitted outside the half-open certification window do not count.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log whose job ran a checkpoint other than the one its model alias certifies do not count.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log whose job was replaced by an approved rerun do not count.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log whose job is listed in the errata job void list do not count.
- Job id values in results_fleet_a.log can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in results_fleet_a.log can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in results_fleet_a.log can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in results_fleet_a.log can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in results_fleet_a.log can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of results_fleet_a.log tagged src=shadow are shadow traffic and are out of scope for certification.
- Where run_registry_export.xml carries a conflicting copy of a results_fleet_a.log fact, the results_fleet_a.log value stands; run_registry_export.xml is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where scorecard_prelim.xlsx carries a conflicting copy of a results_fleet_a.log fact, the results_fleet_a.log value stands; scorecard_prelim.xlsx is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where scores_staging.parquet carries a conflicting copy of a results_fleet_a.log fact, the results_fleet_a.log value stands; scores_staging.parquet is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

review_minutes.docx:
- rerun-approved makes the rerun authoritative and supersedes the original named in the harness events.
- accepted is the routine decision and changes nothing.
- Meeting dates can be written day/month/year.
- Decisions can be written approve, ok, deny, no, exempt.
- rerun-denied, or no decision at all for a rerun, leaves the rerun without effect.
- A table row repeated verbatim in review_minutes.docx is one row.
- exempt-maintenance lets a job submitted inside a maintenance window count.
- Decisions for unregistered jobs are ignored.
- Job id values in review_minutes.docx can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in review_minutes.docx can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in review_minutes.docx can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in review_minutes.docx can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in review_minutes.docx can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Each decision row carries its meeting date; for one job the decision of the latest meeting wins.
- Where a table in review_minutes.docx lists a key twice, the row with the higher revision wins.

run_registry.xml:
- Checkpoint id values in run_registry.xml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Checkpoint id values in run_registry.xml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Checkpoint id values in run_registry.xml can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Checkpoint id values in run_registry.xml can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Checkpoint id values in run_registry.xml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Jobs of the -ema alias name EMA checkpoints, which are registered only in the lineage snapshot.
- Host name values in run_registry.xml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in run_registry.xml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Host name values in run_registry.xml can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Host name values in run_registry.xml can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in run_registry.xml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Job id values in run_registry.xml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in run_registry.xml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in run_registry.xml can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in run_registry.xml can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in run_registry.xml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Model aliases compare case-insensitively.
- Some jobs name the checkpoint by its first 7 characters; the unique checkpoint of the job's model family with that prefix is meant.
- A job whose checkpoint is not the alias's certified checkpoint is stale.
- suite can be a legacy suite name (arithmetic, codegen, ...).
- Where run_registry_export.xml carries a conflicting copy of a run_registry.xml fact, the run_registry.xml value stands; run_registry_export.xml is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl carries a conflicting copy of a run_registry.xml fact, the run_registry.xml value stands; judge_verdicts_rescored.jsonl is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json carries a conflicting copy of a run_registry.xml fact, the run_registry.xml value stands; checkpoint_lineage_snapshot.json is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

run_registry_export.xml:
- Job id values in run_registry_export.xml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in run_registry_export.xml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in run_registry_export.xml can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in run_registry_export.xml can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in run_registry_export.xml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- legacy_no is the only map from retired scheduler numbers (L-nnnnn) to job ids; those numbers appear in legacy_results.txt and harness_events.log.
- Legacy numbers can be written in lower case or without the L- prefix.
- A run_registry_export.xml row that restates a eval_store.db fact is superseded by eval_store.db for that fact.
- A run_registry_export.xml row that restates a harness_events.log fact is superseded by harness_events.log for that fact.
- A run_registry_export.xml row that restates a job_configs.toml fact is superseded by job_configs.toml for that fact.
- A run_registry_export.xml row that restates a manual_overrides.xlsx fact is superseded by manual_overrides.xlsx for that fact.
- A run_registry_export.xml row that restates a results_fleet_a.log fact is superseded by results_fleet_a.log for that fact.
- A run_registry_export.xml row that restates a run_registry.xml fact is superseded by run_registry.xml for that fact.
- Export hosts and checkpoints agree with the run registry for most jobs and differ for some; the run registry wins.
- An export entry identical to the registry entry is not a conflict.
- The export's certified=yes flag predates the approval process and is ignored.
- Export checkpoints are upper-case hex.
- Jobs exported twice are one job.
- The export declares encoding ISO-8859-1.
- Attribute values use entities and CDATA.
- Export hosts are consistent with the old inventory; that consistency does not make them current.
- The export lists more attributes per job (queue, priority, owner); extra attributes do not make it authoritative.
- Durations use a comma decimal mark.
- The export names models by their internal project codes.
- Export entries carry exported_at after the registry's dates; that is the export time, not a newer registration.
- A host of ? means unknown in the export.
- Export rows submitted at the window boundary use an inclusive end; the policy window is half-open.
- Ignoring the export loses the legacy number map; trusting it overrides registry hosts. The precedence rule uses it for legacy numbers only.
- For legacy numbers the export decides the job id; for every other job field the run registry decides.
- Priority values above 100 are real.
- Durations are rounded to minutes and are not used.
- The export's element is task, not job.
- Export shards are 1-based.
- status=complete in the export is a scheduler state and says nothing about certification.
- Export suites use legacy names.
- Export times are US Central (UTC-05:00) without an offset.
- Jobs that appear only in the export were never registered; their result rows are ignored.
- Export rows with cancelled=yes are cancelled submissions.

scorecard_prelim.xlsx:
- Item id values in scorecard_prelim.xlsx can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in scorecard_prelim.xlsx can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in scorecard_prelim.xlsx can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in scorecard_prelim.xlsx can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in scorecard_prelim.xlsx can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- The workbook's window runs to 2025-09-30 inclusive end of day, which matches the half-open policy window.
- Preliminary correctness for multilingual items uses grader g2 for every job.
- Weights adopted by the board apply from the board meeting; the meeting was before the window, so they apply to the whole close.
- Repeated rows are one row.
- Model names in one sheet are joined with semicolons in a single cell.
- Some cells hold non-breaking spaces.
- Preliminary values agree with the rescored judge export and the corrected-gold export; that agreement does not make any of the three authoritative.
- The workbook title says board pack FINAL; the policy ranks it as a preliminary working file.
- Each sheet orders columns differently.
- Model names in the workbook are title-cased.
- Preliminary metrics are computed with naive conventions (micro averages, equal-width calibration bins) and do not match the metric definitions.
- prepared_at is later than every result source; preparation time is not evidence.
- A blank correctness cell means not yet scored.
- Taking the workbook's weights and values together breaks the control reconciliation; dropping the workbook loses the weights; only the weight column is used.
- Items with extreme preliminary scores were flagged for review; the flag has no effect.
- Preliminary scores are percentages.
- Preliminary values are rounded half-up to two decimals.
- A cover-sheet note limits the workbook to discussion use.
- Preliminary per-item correctness agrees with the certified computation for most items and differs for the rest; it is never used as a result.
- Dates are spreadsheet serials in the 1904 date system (days since 1904-01-01).
- Some rows name items retired before the close.
- Rows struck through in the board pack are marked struck=yes and void.
- A scorecard_prelim.xlsx row that restates a intent_predictions.csv fact is superseded by intent_predictions.csv for that fact.
- A scorecard_prelim.xlsx row that restates a legacy_results.txt fact is superseded by legacy_results.txt for that fact.
- A scorecard_prelim.xlsx row that restates a results_fleet_a.log fact is superseded by results_fleet_a.log for that fact.
- A scorecard_prelim.xlsx row that restates a scores_shard1.parquet fact is superseded by scores_shard1.parquet for that fact.
- A scorecard_prelim.xlsx row that restates a scoring_parameters.xlsx fact is superseded by scoring_parameters.xlsx for that fact.
- A scorecard_prelim.xlsx row that restates a suite_manifest.xlsx fact is superseded by suite_manifest.xlsx for that fact.
- suite_weight is the board-adopted weight of the item's suite for the leaderboard aggregate; the weight column is authoritative even though the rest of the workbook is not.
- Weights can be written with a comma decimal mark.
- All items of one suite carry the same suite_weight; that value is the suite's weight.

scores_shard1.parquet:
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet whose job id is not in the run registry (only the retired scheduler export lists it) do not count.
- Host name values in scores_shard1.parquet can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in scores_shard1.parquet can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Host name values in scores_shard1.parquet can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Host name values in scores_shard1.parquet can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in scores_shard1.parquet can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet for items with a counted contamination hit at or above the suite threshold carry no score.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet for items that are non-canonical members of a dedup cluster carry no score.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet for held-out items carry no score; held-out is the last exclusion reason.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet for items retired in the suite manifest carry no score.
- Item id values in scores_shard1.parquet can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in scores_shard1.parquet can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in scores_shard1.parquet can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in scores_shard1.parquet can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in scores_shard1.parquet can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet whose job was submitted inside a maintenance window of its host without an exemption do not count.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet whose job was submitted outside the half-open certification window do not count.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet whose job ran a checkpoint other than the one its model alias certifies do not count.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet whose job was replaced by an approved rerun do not count.
- Rows of scores_shard1.parquet whose job is listed in the errata job void list do not count.
- Job id values in scores_shard1.parquet can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in scores_shard1.parquet can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in scores_shard1.parquet can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in scores_shard1.parquet can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in scores_shard1.parquet can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Where scorecard_prelim.xlsx carries a conflicting copy of a scores_shard1.parquet fact, the scores_shard1.parquet value stands; scorecard_prelim.xlsx is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where scores_staging.parquet carries a conflicting copy of a scores_shard1.parquet fact, the scores_shard1.parquet value stands; scores_staging.parquet is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

- Rows of scores_shard2.parquet whose job was submitted inside a maintenance window of its host without an exemption do not count.
- Rows of scores_shard2.parquet whose job was submitted outside the half-open certification window do not count.
- Rows of scores_shard2.parquet whose job ran a checkpoint other than the one its model alias certifies do not count.

- Rows of scores_shard2.parquet whose job is listed in the errata job void list do not count.
