scores_staging.parquet:
- Host name values in scores_staging.parquet can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in scores_staging.parquet can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Host name values in scores_staging.parquet can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Host name values in scores_staging.parquet can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Host name values in scores_staging.parquet can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of scores_staging.parquet for items with a counted contamination hit at or above the suite threshold carry no score.
- Rows of scores_staging.parquet for items that are non-canonical members of a dedup cluster carry no score.
- Rows of scores_staging.parquet for held-out items carry no score; held-out is the last exclusion reason.
- Rows of scores_staging.parquet for items retired in the suite manifest carry no score.
- Item id values in scores_staging.parquet can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in scores_staging.parquet can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in scores_staging.parquet can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in scores_staging.parquet can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in scores_staging.parquet can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Rows of scores_staging.parquet whose job host was a staging host in its submission week do not count.
- Job id values in scores_staging.parquet can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in scores_staging.parquet can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in scores_staging.parquet can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in scores_staging.parquet can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in scores_staging.parquet can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Staging values agree with production on most keys and differ on the rest; staging values never replace production values.
- A staging row repeated verbatim carries no second result; the repeat still counts as a record line in the source audit.
- Staging values agree with the preliminary scorecard for most items; that agreement is not evidence.
- The staging export metadata says FINAL; the policy's source authority list still ranks it below every production source.
- Staging copies emitted after the job's seal would be late even if staging were authoritative.
- scores_staging.parquet rows are emitted later than the production copies of the same items; later emission does not make a staging row authoritative.
- Ignoring the staging export loses the RESTORE jobs; trusting it for every job replaces production values; only RESTORE jobs use it.
- For RESTORE jobs every field comes from scores_staging.parquet; the field precedence table does not apply to them.
- Staging copies of production jobs name the production host; the host does not make them production rows.
- Staging copies share job, item and sample keys with production rows and are emitted later; the production row still stands.
- Staging emitted values are epoch milliseconds of the staging host clock.
- A scores_staging.parquet row that restates a intent_predictions.csv fact is superseded by intent_predictions.csv for that fact.
- A scores_staging.parquet row that restates a results_fleet_a.log fact is superseded by results_fleet_a.log for that fact.
- A scores_staging.parquet row that restates a scores_shard1.parquet fact is superseded by scores_shard1.parquet for that fact.
- A scores_staging.parquet row that restates a scores_shard2.parquet fact is superseded by scores_shard2.parquet for that fact.

scoring_parameters.xlsx:
- A row repeated verbatim is one row.
- Entries of scoring_parameters.xlsx whose item id is not registered in item_registry.csv are ignored.
- Item id values in scoring_parameters.xlsx can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in scoring_parameters.xlsx can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in scoring_parameters.xlsx can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in scoring_parameters.xlsx can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in scoring_parameters.xlsx can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Where gold_labels_corrected.csv carries a conflicting copy of a scoring_parameters.xlsx fact, the scoring_parameters.xlsx value stands; gold_labels_corrected.csv is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
- Where scorecard_prelim.xlsx carries a conflicting copy of a scoring_parameters.xlsx fact, the scoring_parameters.xlsx value stands; scorecard_prelim.xlsx is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

slice_attributes.csv:
- An entry of slice_attributes.csv repeated verbatim is one entry, not a conflict.
- Entries of slice_attributes.csv whose item id is not registered in item_registry.csv are ignored.
- Item id values in slice_attributes.csv can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in slice_attributes.csv can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in slice_attributes.csv can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in slice_attributes.csv can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in slice_attributes.csv can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.

slice_definitions.yaml:
- The region code NA is North America; it is not a missing value.
- Region values are case-insensitive.
- A region entry repeated verbatim is one entry.
- Entries of slice_definitions.yaml whose item id is not registered in item_registry.csv are ignored.
- Item id values in slice_definitions.yaml can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in slice_definitions.yaml can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in slice_definitions.yaml can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in slice_definitions.yaml can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in slice_definitions.yaml can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- Where locale_codes_legacy.tsv carries a conflicting copy of a slice_definitions.yaml fact, the slice_definitions.yaml value stands; locale_codes_legacy.tsv is authoritative only for the part the policy names.

source_conventions.md:
- Some cells in source_conventions.md tables are wrapped in double quotes; the quotes are not part of the value.
- A table row repeated verbatim in source_conventions.md is one row.
- Pipes inside table cells are escaped as \|.
- Routing rows for unregistered jobs are ignored.
- A continuation table in source_conventions.md repeats the header with its columns reordered.
- Job id values in source_conventions.md can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in source_conventions.md can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Job id values in source_conventions.md can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Job id values in source_conventions.md can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Job id values in source_conventions.md can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- The first cell of some source_conventions.md table rows ends with a no-break space.
- A copy source of - means the job has no second copy.
- Where a table in source_conventions.md lists a key twice, the row with the higher revision wins.
- Revision values in source_conventions.md can be zero-padded (03 means 3).
- Revision values in source_conventions.md can be written with an r prefix (r3 means 3).
- Source names can be written without extension or in upper case.
- Cells of source_conventions.md can carry trailing spaces; strip them.

suite_manifest.xlsx:
- Status added marks an item introduced this quarter; it is active, not pending.
- Each sheet lists only its own suites; a row whose item prefix belongs to another sheet's suites is a misfiled row and is ignored.
- A row repeated verbatim is one row.
- Entries of suite_manifest.xlsx whose item id is not registered in item_registry.csv are ignored.
- Item id values in suite_manifest.xlsx can be written in the wrong letter case; compare them in the canonical case of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in suite_manifest.xlsx can use full-width digits; apply Unicode NFKC before joining.
- Item id values in suite_manifest.xlsx can carry a Cyrillic look-alike letter from the confusables table; map it back to Latin before joining.
- Item id values in suite_manifest.xlsx can lose their zero padding; restore the fixed digit width of the identifier grammar.
- Item id values in suite_manifest.xlsx can carry leading or trailing spaces or no-break spaces; strip them before joining.
- A retirement takes effect only when changed_at is before the window end (2025-10-01T00:00:00Z); later retirements do not apply to this close.
- An item retired and later reinstated (a higher revision with status active) is active.
- A changed_at of 0 means the date was not captured; such rows take effect at the start of the window.
- Status can be written retired, ret or R; active, act or A; added means active.
- Where scorecard_prelim.xlsx carries a conflicting copy of a suite_manifest.xlsx fact, the suite_manifest.xlsx value stands; scorecard_prelim.xlsx is authoritative only for the part the policy names.
